>> import asyncio
>> from pathlib import Path
>> from datetime import datetime
>> from bleak import BleakClient, BleakScanner
>>
>> async def main():
>>     print("HAYLOU LS11 — READ ONLY INSPECTOR")
>>     print("Only reading GATT characteristics. No writes.\n")
>>
>>     devices = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=12, return_adv=True)
>>     found = []
>>
>>     for address, pair in devices.items():
>>         device, adv = pair
>>         name = (adv.local_name or device.name or "").upper()
>>         if "HAYLOU" in name or "LS11" in name:
>>             found.append((device, name))
>>
>>     if not found:
>>         print("Haylou not found. Try again with the watch awake.")
>>         return
>>
>>     for i, (device, name) in enumerate(found, 1):
>>         print(f"[{i}] {name} | {device.address}")
>>
>>     choice = int(input("\nSelect watch number: "))
>>     device, name = found[choice - 1]
>>
>>     report = [
>>         "HAYLOU LS11 GATT READ REPORT",
>>         f"Date: {datetime.now().isoformat()}",
>>         f"Device: {name}",
>>         f"Address: {device.address}",
>>         ""
>>     ]
>>
>>     print("\nConnecting...")
>>
>>     async with BleakClient(device, timeout=25) as client:
>>         print("Connected:", client.is_connected)
>>
>>         for service in client.services:
>>             header = f"\nSERVICE: {service.uuid}"
>>             print(header)
>>             report.append(header)
>>
>>             for char in service.characteristics:
>>                 line = f"  CHAR: {char.uuid} | {char.properties}"
>>                 print(line)
>>                 report.append(line)
>>
>>                 if "read" not in char.properties:
>>                     continue
>>
>>                 try:
>>                     data = bytes(await client.read_gatt_char(char))
>>                     hx = data.hex(" ").upper() or "<EMPTY>"
>>                     ascii_value = "".join(
>>                         chr(b) if 32 <= b <= 126 else "." for b in data
>>                     )
>>                     result = f"    READ: {hx}"
>>                     print(result)
>>                     report.append(result)
>>
>>                     result = f"    ASCII: {ascii_value}"
>>                     print(result)
>>                     report.append(result)
>>
>>                 except Exception as e:
>>                     result = f"    READ ERROR: {e}"
>>                     print(result)
>>                     report.append(result)
>>
>>     path = Path.home() / "Desktop" / "ls11_gatt_read_report.txt"
>>     path.write_text("\n".join(report), encoding="utf-8")
>>
>>     print("\nREPORT SAVED:", path)
>>     print("Send the report contents here.")
>>
>> asyncio.run(main())
>> '@

PS C:\Users\User> $code = @'
>> import asyncio
>> from datetime import datetime
>> from pathlib import Path
>> from bleak import BleakClient, BleakScanner
>>
>> DURATION = 80
>>
>> async def main():
>>     print("HAYLOU LS11 — PASSIVE BLE MONITOR")
>>     print("No firmware writes. No application commands.")
>>     print("Close Haylou Fun and turn off Bluetooth on iPhone.\n")
>>
>>     devices = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=12, return_adv=True)
>>     matches = []
>>
>>     for _, pair in devices.items():
>>         device, adv = pair
>>         name = (adv.local_name or device.name or "").strip()
>>         if "HAYLOU" in name.upper() or "RS4 PLUS" in name.upper():
>>             matches.append((device, name))
>>
>>     if not matches:
>>         print("Watch not found. Wake the screen and retry.")
>>         return
>>
>>     for i, (device, name) in enumerate(matches, 1):
>>         print(f"[{i}] {name} | {device.address}")
>>
>>     selected = int(input("\nSelect watch: "))
>>     if selected < 1 or selected > len(matches):
>>         print("Invalid selection")
>>         return
>>
>>     device, name = matches[selected - 1]
>>     events = []
>>     started = None
>>
>>     def make_handler(uuid):
>>         def handler(sender, data):
>>             raw = bytes(data)
>>             elapsed = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() - started
>>             hx = raw.hex(" ").upper()
>>             ascii_data = "".join(
>>                 chr(b) if 32 <= b <= 126 else "." for b in raw
>>             )
>>             events.append((elapsed, uuid, hx, ascii_data))
>>             print(f"[{elapsed:6.2f}s] {uuid} | {hx} | {ascii_data}")
>>         return handler
>>
>>     print("\nConnecting...")
>>
>>     try:
>>         async with BleakClient(device, timeout=25) as client:
>>             print("Connected:", client.is_connected)
>>
>>             started = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
>>             subscribed = []
>>
>>             for service in client.services:
>>                 for char in service.characteristics:
>>                     if not ({"notify", "indicate"} & set(char.properties)):
>>                         continue
>>
>>                     try:
>>                         await client.start_notify(
>>                             char, make_handler(str(char.uuid))
>>                         )
>>                         subscribed.append(char)
>>                         print("Subscribed:", char.uuid)
>>                     except Exception as exc:
>>                         print("Skipped:", char.uuid, exc)
>>
>>             if not subscribed:
>>                 print("No notification channels available.")
>>                 return
>>
>>             print("\n80-second recording started.")
>>             prompts = [
>>                 (0, "Do not touch the watch."),
>>                 (10, "Wake the screen."),
>>                 (25, "Swipe down once, then return home."),
>>                 (40, "Swipe up once, then return home."),
>>                 (55, "Open heart rate screen. Do not start firmware update."),
>>                 (70, "Return to home screen.")
>>             ]
>>
>>             index = 0
>>             loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
>>
>>             while loop.time() - started < DURATION:
>>                 elapsed = loop.time() - started
>>
>>                 if index < len(prompts) and elapsed >= prompts[index][0]:
>>                     t, message = prompts[index]
>>                     print(f"\n[T+{t}s] {message}")
>>                     index += 1
>>
>>                 await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
>>
>>             for char in subscribed:
>>                 try:
>>                     await client.stop_notify(char)
>>                 except Exception:
>>                     pass
>>
>>     except Exception as exc:
>>         print("Connection error:", repr(exc))
>>
>>     report = [
>>         "HAYLOU LS11 PASSIVE BLE REPORT",
>>         f"Date: {datetime.now().isoformat()}",
>>         f"Device: {name}",
>>         f"MAC: {device.address}",
>>         f"Events: {len(events)}",
>>         "",
>>     ]
>>
>>     for elapsed, uuid, hx, asc in events:
>>         report.append(
>>             f"[{elapsed:.3f}s] {uuid} | {hx} | ASCII={asc}"
>>         )
>>
>>     output = Path.home() / "Desktop" / "ls11_passive_ble_report.txt"
>>     output.write_text("\n".join(report), encoding="utf-8")
>>
>>     print("\nREPORT SAVED:", output)
>>     print("Total packets:", len(events))
>>
>> if __name__ == "__main__":
>>     asyncio.run(main())
>> '@
